ZW3D, an integrated CAD/CAM solution designed for the whole process of product development, features the fastest kernel for Solid-Surface Hybrid Modeling, non-solid mold parting and smart CNC machining strategies from 2-axis to 5-axis. It brings customers multiple benefits such as reduced costs, optimized design workflow and improved efficiency.==>제품 개발의 전체 과정을 고려하여 설계된 통합 CAD/CAM 솔루션인 ZW3D는 솔리드 표면 하이브리드 모델링을 위한 가장 빠른 커널, 솔리드 외의 몰드 파팅, 2축~5축 스마트 CNC 기계 가공 전략이 특징입니다. 고객에게는 비용 절감, 설계 워크플로 최적화, 효율 개선 등 다양한 장점이 있습니다.

ZW3D, an integrated CAD/CAM solution designed for the whole process of product development, features the fastest kernel for Solid-Surface Hybrid Modeling, non-solid mold parting and smart CNC machining strategies from 2-axis to 5-axis. It brings customers multiple benefits such as reduced costs, optimized design workflow and improved efficiency.==>ZW3D ist eine integrierte CAD/CAM-Lösung für den gesamten Produktentwicklungsprozess, bietet den schnellsten Kernel für Solid-Surface Hybrid Modelling, nicht-massiven Formenbau und intelligente CNC-Bearbeitungsstrategien von 2 bis 5 Achsen. Sie bringt den Kunden zahlreiche Vorteile wie Kostenreduzierung, optimierte Konstruktionsabläufe und gesteigerte Leistungsfähigkeit.

ZW3D, an integrated CAD/CAM solution designed for the whole process of product development, features the fastest kernel for Solid-Surface Hybrid Modeling, non-solid mold parting and smart CNC machining strategies from 2-axis to 5-axis. It brings customers multiple benefits such as reduced costs, optimized design workflow and improved efficiency.==>製品開発の全プロセス向けに設計された統合CAD / CAMソリューションであるZW3Dは、ソリッドサーフェスハイブリッドモデリング、非ソリッドモールドパーティング、2軸から5軸までのスマートCNC機械加工戦略向けの最速カーネルを備えています。これにより、コストの削減、設計ワークフローの最適化、効率の向上など、複数のメリットをもたらします。

Open in ZW3D==>Открыть в ZW3D
ZW3D, an integrated CAD/CAM solution designed for the whole process of product development, features the fastest kernel for Solid-Surface Hybrid Modeling, non-solid mold parting and smart CNC machining strategies from 2-axis to 5-axis. It brings customers multiple benefits such as reduced costs, optimized design workflow and improved efficiency.==>ZW3D, универсальное CAD/CAM решение для выполнения всего процесса разработки продукции, имеющее максимально быстрое ядро для выполнения Гибридного моделирования, работы с пресс-формами и интеллектуальными стратегиями 2-5 осевой фрезерной обработкой на станках с ЧПУ. Предоставляет множество преимуществ, таких как снижение затрат, оптимизацию рабочего процесса проектирования и повышение эффективности.
